Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Add CLI Version to Checkpoint and Session Metadata JSONs

## Approach

Create a `buildinfo` package to hold `Version`/`Commit` (breaking the import cycle). Add a `CLIVersion` field to both `CheckpointSummary` and `session.State`, populated from `buildinfo.Version`.

## Changes

### 1. New file: `cmd/entire/cli/buildinfo/buildinfo.go`

```go
package buildinfo

var (
    Version = "dev"
    Commit  = "unknown"
)
```

### 2. `cmd/entire/cli/root.go` — use `buildinfo`

- Remove `Version = "dev"` and `Commit = "unknown"` vars (lines 29-32)
- Import `buildinfo`, replace `Version` → `buildinfo.Version`, `Commit` → `buildinfo.Commit`

### 3. Build files — update ldflags from `cli.Version` → `cli/buildinfo.Version`

- `mise.toml` (line 35)
- `.goreleaser.yaml` (lines 21-22)
- `mise-tasks/dev/publish` (line 12)

### 4. `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/checkpoint.go`

Add `CLIVersion string` to `CheckpointSummary` (line ~410):
```go
CLIVersion string `json:"cli_version,omitempty"`
```

### 5. `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/committed.go` — populate in `writeCheckpointSummary`

Import `buildinfo`. At line 378:
```go
CLIVersion: buildinfo.Version,
```

### 6. `cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go`

Add field to `State`:
```go
CLIVersion string `json:"cli_version,omitempty"`
```

### 7. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go` — set on init

Import `buildinfo`. In `initializeSession()` (line 219):
```go
CLIVersion: buildinfo.Version,
```

### 8. `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go` — set on init

Import `buildinfo`. In session creation (line 945):
```go
CLIVersion: buildinfo.Version,
```

    34→func NewRootCmd() *cobra.Command {
    35→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    36→		Use:   "entire",
    37→		Short: "Entire CLI",
    38→		Long:  "A command-line interface for Entire" + gettingStarted + accessibilityHelp,
    39→		// Let main.go handle error printing to avoid duplication
    40→		SilenceErrors: true,
    41→		SilenceUsage:  true,
    42→		// Hide completion command from help but keep it functional
    43→		CompletionOptions: cobra.CompletionOptions{
    44→			HiddenDefaultCmd: true,
    45→		},
    46→		PersistentPostRun: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) {
    47→			// Skip for hidden commands
    48→			if cmd.Hidden {
    49→				return
    50→			}
    51→
    52→			// Load settings once for telemetry and version check
    53→			var telemetryEnabled *bool
    54→			settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
    55→			if err == nil {
    56→				telemetryEnabled = settings.Telemetry
    57→			}
    58→
    59→			// Check if telemetry is enabled
    60→			if telemetryEnabled != nil && *telemetryEnabled {
    61→				// Use detached tracking (non-blocking)
    62→				installedAgents := GetAgentsWithHooksInstalled()
    63→				agentStr := JoinAgentNames(installedAgents)
    64→				telemetry.TrackCommandDetached(cmd, settings.Strategy, agentStr, settings.Enabled, Version)
    65→			}
    66→
    67→			// Version check and notification (synchronous with 2s timeout)
    68→			// Runs AFTER command completes to avoid interfering with interactive modes
    69→			versioncheck.CheckAndNotify(cmd, Version)
    70→		},
    71→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    72→			return cmd.Help()
    73→		},
    74→	}

    78→	cmd.AddCommand(newResumeCmd())
    79→	cmd.AddCommand(newCleanCmd())

    98→func newVersionCmd() *cobra.Command {
    99→	return &cobra.Command{
   100→		Use:   "version",
   101→		Short: "Show version information",
   102→		Run: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) {

   104→			fmt.Printf("Go version: %s\n", runtime.Version())
   105→			fmt.Printf("OS/Arch: %s/%s\n", runtime.GOOS, runtime.GOARCH)
   106→		},
   107→	}
   108→}

   110→// newSendAnalyticsCmd creates the hidden command for sending analytics from a detached subprocess.
   111→// This command is invoked by TrackCommandDetached and should not be called directly by users.
   112→func newSendAnalyticsCmd() *cobra.Command {
   113→	return &cobra.Command{
   114→		Use:    "__send_analytics",
   115→		Hidden: true,
   116→		Args:   cobra.ExactArgs(1),
   117→		Run: func(_ *cobra.Command, args []string) {
   118→			telemetry.SendEvent(args[0])
   119→		},
   120→	}
   121→}

     1→// Package checkpoint provides types and interfaces for checkpoint storage.
     2→//
     3→// A Checkpoint captures a point-in-time within a session, containing either
     4→// full state (Temporary) or metadata with a commit reference (Committed).
     5→//
     6→// See docs/architecture/sessions-and-checkpoints.md for the full domain model.
     7→package checkpoint
     8→
     9→import (
    10→	"context"
    11→	"errors"
    12→	"time"
    13→
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/id"
    16→
    17→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
    18→)
    19→
    20→// Errors returned by checkpoint operations.
    21→var (
    22→	// ErrCheckpointNotFound is returned when a checkpoint ID doesn't exist.
    23→	ErrCheckpointNotFound = errors.New("checkpoint not found")
    24→
    25→	// ErrNoTranscript is returned when a checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
    26→	ErrNoTranscript = errors.New("no transcript found for checkpoint")
    27→)
    28→
    29→// Checkpoint represents a save point within a session.
    30→type Checkpoint struct {
    31→	// ID is the unique checkpoint identifier
    32→	ID string
    33→
    34→	// SessionID is the session this checkpoint belongs to
    35→	SessionID string
    36→
    37→	// Timestamp is when this checkpoint was created
    38→	Timestamp time.Time
    39→
    40→	// Type indicates temporary (full state) or committed (metadata only)
    41→	Type Type
    42→
    43→	// Message is a human-readable description of the checkpoint
    44→	Message string
    45→}
    46→
    47→// Type indicates the storage location and lifecycle of a checkpoint.
    48→type Type int
    49→
    50→const (
    51→	// Temporary checkpoints contain full state (code + metadata) and are stored
    52→	// on shadow branches (entire/<commit-hash>). Used for intra-session rewind.
    53→	Temporary Type = iota
    54→
    55→	// Committed checkpoints contain metadata + commit reference and are stored
    56→	// on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch. They are the permanent record.
    57→	Committed
    58→)
    59→
    60→// Store provides low-level primitives for reading and writing checkpoints.
    61→// This is used by strategies to implement their storage approach.
    62→//
    63→// The interface matches the GitStore implementation signatures directly:
    64→// - WriteTemporary takes WriteTemporaryOptions and returns a result with commit hash and skip status
    65→// - ReadTemporary takes baseCommit (not sessionID) since shadow branches are keyed by commit
    66→// - List methods return implementation-specific info types for richer data
    67→type Store interface {
    68→	// WriteTemporary writes a temporary checkpoint (full state) to a shadow branch.
    69→	// Shadow branches are named entire/<base-commit-short-hash>.
    70→	// Returns a result containing the commit hash and whether the checkpoint was skipped.
    71→	// Checkpoints are skipped (deduplicated) when the tree hash matches the previous checkpoint.
    72→	WriteTemporary(ctx context.Context, opts WriteTemporaryOptions) (WriteTemporaryResult, error)
    73→
    74→	// ReadTemporary reads the latest checkpoint from a shadow branch.
    75→	// baseCommit is the commit hash the session is based on.
    76→	// worktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree).
    77→	// Returns nil, nil if the shadow branch doesn't exist.
    78→	ReadTemporary(ctx context.Context, baseCommit, worktreeID string) (*ReadTemporaryResult, error)
    79→
    80→	// ListTemporary lists all shadow branches with their checkpoint info.
    81→	ListTemporary(ctx context.Context) ([]TemporaryInfo, error)
    82→
    83→	// WriteCommitted writes a committed checkpoint to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    84→	// Checkpoints are stored at sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    85→	WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error
    86→
    87→	// ReadCommitted reads a committed checkpoint's summary by ID.
    88→	// Returns only the CheckpointSummary (paths + aggregated stats), not actual content.
    89→	// Use ReadSessionContent to read actual transcript/prompts/context.
    90→	// Returns nil, nil if the checkpoint does not exist.
    91→	ReadCommitted(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*CheckpointSummary, error)
    92→
    93→	// ReadSessionContent reads the actual content for a specific session within a checkpoint.
    94→	// sessionIndex is 0-based (0 for first session, 1 for second, etc.).
    95→	// Returns the session's metadata, transcript, prompts, and context.
    96→	ReadSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionIndex int) (*SessionContent, error)
    97→
    98→	// ReadSessionContentByID reads a session's content by its session ID.
    99→	// Useful when you have the session ID but don't know its index within the checkpoint.
   100→	ReadSessionContentByID(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string) (*SessionContent, error)
   101→
   102→	// ListCommitted lists all committed checkpoints.
   103→	ListCommitted(ctx context.Context) ([]CommittedInfo, error)
   104→}
   105→
   106→// WriteTemporaryResult contains the result of writing a temporary checkpoint.
   107→type WriteTemporaryResult struct {
   108→	// CommitHash is the hash of the created or existing checkpoint commit
   109→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   110→
   111→	// Skipped is true if the checkpoint was skipped due to no changes
   112→	// (tree hash matched the previous checkpoint)
   113→	Skipped bool
   114→}
   115→
   116→// WriteTemporaryOptions contains options for writing a temporary checkpoint.
   117→type WriteTemporaryOptions struct {
   118→	// SessionID is the session identifier
   119→	SessionID string
   120→
   121→	// BaseCommit is the commit hash this session is based on
   122→	BaseCommit string
   123→
   124→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
   125→	// Used to create worktree-specific shadow branch names
   126→	WorktreeID string
   127→
   128→	// ModifiedFiles are files that have been modified (relative paths)
   129→	ModifiedFiles []string
   130→
   131→	// NewFiles are files that have been created (relative paths)
   132→	NewFiles []string
   133→
   134→	// DeletedFiles are files that have been deleted (relative paths)
   135→	DeletedFiles []string
   136→
   137→	// MetadataDir is the relative path to the metadata directory
   138→	MetadataDir string
   139→
   140→	// MetadataDirAbs is the absolute path to the metadata directory
   141→	MetadataDirAbs string
   142→
   143→	// CommitMessage is the commit subject line
   144→	CommitMessage string
   145→
   146→	// AuthorName is the name to use for commits
   147→	AuthorName string
   148→
   149→	// AuthorEmail is the email to use for commits
   150→	AuthorEmail string
   151→
   152→	// IsFirstCheckpoint indicates if this is the first checkpoint of the session
   153→	// When true, all working directory files are captured (not just modified)
   154→	IsFirstCheckpoint bool
   155→}
   156→
   157→// ReadTemporaryResult contains the result of reading a temporary checkpoint.
   158→type ReadTemporaryResult struct {
   159→	// CommitHash is the hash of the checkpoint commit
   160→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   161→
   162→	// TreeHash is the hash of the tree containing the checkpoint state
   163→	TreeHash plumbing.Hash
   164→
   165→	// SessionID is the session identifier from the commit trailer
   166→	SessionID string
   167→
   168→	// MetadataDir is the metadata directory path from the commit trailer
   169→	MetadataDir string
   170→
   171→	// Timestamp is when the checkpoint was created
   172→	Timestamp time.Time
   173→}
   174→
   175→// TemporaryInfo contains summary information about a shadow branch.
   176→type TemporaryInfo struct {
   177→	// BranchName is the full branch name (e.g., "entire/abc1234")
   178→	BranchName string
   179→
   180→	// BaseCommit is the short commit hash this branch is based on
   181→	BaseCommit string
   182→
   183→	// LatestCommit is the hash of the latest commit on the branch
   184→	LatestCommit plumbing.Hash
   185→
   186→	// SessionID is the session identifier from the latest commit
   187→	SessionID string
   188→
   189→	// Timestamp is when the latest checkpoint was created
   190→	Timestamp time.Time
   191→}
   192→
   193→// WriteCommittedOptions contains options for writing a committed checkpoint.
   194→type WriteCommittedOptions struct {
   195→	// CheckpointID is the stable 12-hex-char identifier
   196→	CheckpointID id.CheckpointID
   197→
   198→	// SessionID is the session identifier
   199→	SessionID string
   200→
   201→	// Strategy is the name of the strategy that created this checkpoint
   202→	Strategy string
   203→
   204→	// Branch is the branch name where the checkpoint was created (empty if detached HEAD)
   205→	Branch string
   206→
   207→	// Transcript is the session transcript content (full.jsonl)
   208→	Transcript []byte
   209→
   210→	// Prompts contains user prompts from the session
   211→	Prompts []string
   212→
   213→	// Context is the generated context.md content
   214→	Context []byte
   215→
   216→	// FilesTouched are files modified during the session
   217→	FilesTouched []string
   218→
   219→	// CheckpointsCount is the number of checkpoints in this session
   220→	CheckpointsCount int
   221→
   222→	// EphemeralBranch is the shadow branch name (for manual-commit strategy)
   223→	EphemeralBranch string
   224→
   225→	// AuthorName is the name to use for commits
   226→	AuthorName string
   227→
   228→	// AuthorEmail is the email to use for commits
   229→	AuthorEmail string
   230→
   231→	// MetadataDir is a directory containing additional metadata files to copy
   232→	// If set, all files in this directory will be copied to the checkpoint path
   233→	// This is useful for copying task metadata files, subagent transcripts, etc.
   234→	MetadataDir string
   235→
   236→	// Task checkpoint fields (for auto-commit strategy task checkpoints)
   237→	IsTask    bool   // Whether this is a task checkpoint
   238→	ToolUseID string // Tool use ID for task checkpoints
   239→
   240→	// Additional task checkpoint fields for subagent checkpoints
   241→	AgentID                string // Subagent identifier
   242→	CheckpointUUID         string // UUID for transcript truncation when rewinding
   243→	TranscriptPath         string // Path to session transcript file (alternative to in-memory Transcript)
   244→	SubagentTranscriptPath string // Path to subagent's transcript file
   245→
   246→	// Incremental checkpoint fields
   247→	IsIncremental       bool   // Whether this is an incremental checkpoint
   248→	IncrementalSequence int    // Checkpoint sequence number
   249→	IncrementalType     string // Tool type that triggered this checkpoint
   250→	IncrementalData     []byte // Tool input payload for this checkpoint
   251→
   252→	// Commit message fields (used for task checkpoints)
   253→	CommitSubject string // Subject line for the metadata commit (overrides default)
   254→
   255→	// Agent identifies the agent that created this checkpoint (e.g., "Claude Code", "Cursor")
   256→	Agent agent.AgentType
   257→
   258→	// Transcript position at checkpoint start - tracks what was added during this checkpoint
   259→	TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string // Last identifier when checkpoint started (UUID for Claude, message ID for Gemini)
   260→	CheckpointTranscriptStart   int    // Transcript line offset at start of this checkpoint's data
   261→
   262→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart is written to both CommittedMetadata.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   263→	// and the deprecated CommittedMetadata.TranscriptLinesAtStart for backward compatibility.
   264→
   265→	// TokenUsage contains the token usage for this checkpoint
   266→	TokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   267→
   268→	// InitialAttribution is line-level attribution calculated at commit time
   269→	// comparing checkpoint tree (agent work) to committed tree (may include human edits)
   270→	InitialAttribution *InitialAttribution
   271→
   272→	// Summary is an optional AI-generated summary for this checkpoint.
   273→	// This field may be nil when:

   275→	//   - summary generation failed (non-blocking, logged as warning)
   276→	//   - the transcript was empty or too short to summarize
   277→	//   - the checkpoint predates the summarization feature
   278→	Summary *Summary
   279→}

   281→// CommittedInfo contains summary information about a committed checkpoint.
   282→type CommittedInfo struct {
   283→	// CheckpointID is the stable 12-hex-char identifier

   285→
   286→	// SessionID is the session identifier (most recent session for multi-session checkpoints)

   291→
   292→	// CheckpointsCount is the total number of checkpoints across all sessions

   295→	// FilesTouched are files modified during all sessions

   297→
   298→	// Agent identifies the agent that created this checkpoint

   303→
   304→	// ToolUseID is the tool use ID for task checkpoints
   305→	ToolUseID string

   307→	// Multi-session support

   348→	CheckpointTranscriptStart   int    `json:"checkpoint_transcript_start,omitempty"`    // Transcript line offset at start of this checkpoint's data

   362→
   363→// GetTranscriptStart returns the transcript line offset at which this checkpoint's data begins.
   364→// Returns 0 for new checkpoints (start from beginning). For data written by older CLI versions,
   365→// falls back to the deprecated TranscriptLinesAtStart field.
   366→func (m CommittedMetadata) GetTranscriptStart() int {
   367→	if m.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   368→		return m.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   369→	}
   370→	return m.TranscriptLinesAtStart
   371→}

   374→// Paths include the full checkpoint path prefix (e.g., "/a1/b2c3d4e5f6/1/metadata.json").

   378→	Transcript  string `json:"transcript"`
   379→	Context     string `json:"context"`
   380→	ContentHash string `json:"content_hash"`
   381→	Prompt      string `json:"prompt"`
   382→}
   383→
   384→// CheckpointSummary is the root-level metadata.json for a checkpoint.
   385→// It contains aggregated statistics from all sessions and a map of session IDs
   386→// to their file paths. Session-specific data (including initial_attribution)
   387→// is stored in the session's subdirectory metadata.json.
   388→//
   389→// Structure on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch:
   390→//
   391→//	<checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/
   392→//	├── metadata.json         # This CheckpointSummary
   393→//	├── 1/                    # First session
   394→//	│   ├── metadata.json     # Session-specific CommittedMetadata
   395→//	│   ├── full.jsonl
   396→//	│   ├── prompt.txt
   397→//	│   ├── context.md
   398→//	│   └── content_hash.txt
   399→//	├── 2/                    # Second session
   400→//	└── 3/                    # Third session...
   401→//
   402→//nolint:revive // Named CheckpointSummary to avoid conflict with existing Summary struct
   403→type CheckpointSummary struct {
   404→	CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID    `json:"checkpoint_id"`
   405→	Strategy         string             `json:"strategy"`
   406→	Branch           string             `json:"branch,omitempty"`
   407→	CheckpointsCount int                `json:"checkpoints_count"`
   408→	FilesTouched     []string           `json:"files_touched"`
   409→	Sessions         []SessionFilePaths `json:"sessions"`
   410→	TokenUsage       *agent.TokenUsage  `json:"token_usage,omitempty"`
   411→}
   412→
   413→// Summary contains AI-generated summary of a checkpoint.
   414→type Summary struct {
   415→	Intent    string           `json:"intent"`     // What user wanted to accomplish
   416→	Outcome   string           `json:"outcome"`    // What was achieved
   417→	Learnings LearningsSummary `json:"learnings"`  // Categorized learnings
   418→	Friction  []string         `json:"friction"`   // Problems/annoyances encountered
   419→	OpenItems []string         `json:"open_items"` // Tech debt, unfinished work
   420→}
   421→
   422→// LearningsSummary contains learnings grouped by scope.
   423→type LearningsSummary struct {
   424→	Repo     []string       `json:"repo"`     // Codebase-specific patterns/conventions
   425→	Code     []CodeLearning `json:"code"`     // File/module specific findings
   426→	Workflow []string       `json:"workflow"` // General dev practices
   427→}
   428→
   429→// CodeLearning captures a learning tied to a specific code location.
   430→type CodeLearning struct {
   431→	Path    string `json:"path"`               // File path
   432→	Line    int    `json:"line,omitempty"`     // Start line number
   433→	EndLine int    `json:"end_line,omitempty"` // End line for ranges (optional)
   434→	Finding string `json:"finding"`            // What was learned
   435→}
   436→
   437→// InitialAttribution captures line-level attribution metrics at commit time.
   438→// This is a point-in-time snapshot comparing the checkpoint tree (agent work)
   439→// against the committed tree (may include human edits).
   440→//
   441→// Attribution Metrics:
   442→//   - TotalCommitted measures "net additions" (lines added that remain in the commit)
   443→//   - AgentPercentage represents "of the new code added, what percentage came from the agent"
   444→//   - Deletion work is tracked separately in HumanRemoved (not included in percentage)
   445→//
   446→// Deletion-Only Commits:
   447→// For commits with only deletions (no additions), TotalCommitted will be 0 and
   448→// AgentPercentage will be 0. This is by design - the percentage metric is only
   449→// meaningful for commits that add code. Deletion contributions are captured in
   450→// the HumanRemoved field but don't affect the attribution percentage.
   451→type InitialAttribution struct {
   452→	CalculatedAt    time.Time `json:"calculated_at"`
   453→	AgentLines      int       `json:"agent_lines"`      // Lines added by agent (base → shadow diff)
   454→	HumanAdded      int       `json:"human_added"`      // Lines added by human (excluding modifications)
   455→	HumanModified   int       `json:"human_modified"`   // Lines modified by human (estimate: min(added, removed))
   456→	HumanRemoved    int       `json:"human_removed"`    // Lines removed by human (excluding modifications)
   457→	TotalCommitted  int       `json:"total_committed"`  // Net additions in commit (agent + human new lines, not total file size)
   458→	AgentPercentage float64   `json:"agent_percentage"` // agent_lines / total_committed * 100 (0 for deletion-only commits)
   459→}
   460→
   461→// Info provides summary information for listing checkpoints.
   462→// This is the generic checkpoint info type.
   463→type Info struct {
   464→	// ID is the checkpoint identifier
   465→	ID string
   466→
   467→	// SessionID identifies the session
   468→	SessionID string
   469→
   470→	// Type indicates temporary or committed
   471→	Type Type
   472→
   473→	// CreatedAt is when the checkpoint was created
   474→	CreatedAt time.Time
   475→
   476→	// Message is a summary description
   477→	Message string
   478→}
   479→
   480→// WriteTemporaryTaskOptions contains options for writing a task checkpoint.
   481→// Task checkpoints are created when a subagent completes and contain both
   482→// code changes and task-specific metadata.
   483→type WriteTemporaryTaskOptions struct {
   484→	// SessionID is the session identifier
   485→	SessionID string
   486→
   487→	// BaseCommit is the commit hash this session is based on
   488→	BaseCommit string
   489→
   490→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
   491→	// Used to create worktree-specific shadow branch names
   492→	WorktreeID string
   493→
   494→	// ToolUseID is the unique identifier for this Task tool invocation
   495→	ToolUseID string
   496→
   497→	// AgentID is the subagent identifier
   498→	AgentID string
   499→
   500→	// ModifiedFiles are files that have been modified (relative paths)
   501→	ModifiedFiles []string
   502→
   503→	// NewFiles are files that have been created (relative paths)
   504→	NewFiles []string
   505→
   506→	// DeletedFiles are files that have been deleted (relative paths)
   507→	DeletedFiles []string
   508→
   509→	// TranscriptPath is the path to the main session transcript
   510→	TranscriptPath string
   511→
   512→	// SubagentTranscriptPath is the path to the subagent's transcript
   513→	SubagentTranscriptPath string
   514→
   515→	// CheckpointUUID is the UUID for transcript truncation when rewinding
   516→	CheckpointUUID string
   517→
   518→	// CommitMessage is the commit message (already formatted)
   519→	CommitMessage string
   520→
   521→	// AuthorName is the name to use for commits
   522→	AuthorName string
   523→
   524→	// AuthorEmail is the email to use for commits
   525→	AuthorEmail string
   526→
   527→	// IsIncremental indicates this is an incremental checkpoint
   528→	IsIncremental bool
   529→
   530→	// IncrementalSequence is the checkpoint sequence number
   531→	IncrementalSequence int
   532→
   533→	// IncrementalType is the tool that triggered this checkpoint
   534→	IncrementalType string
   535→
   536→	// IncrementalData is the tool_input payload for this checkpoint
   537→	IncrementalData []byte
   538→}
   539→
   540→// TemporaryCheckpointInfo contains information about a single commit on a shadow branch.
   541→// Used by ListTemporaryCheckpoints to provide rewind point data.
   542→type TemporaryCheckpointInfo struct {
   543→	// CommitHash is the hash of the checkpoint commit
   544→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   545→
   546→	// Message is the first line of the commit message
   547→	Message string
   548→
   549→	// SessionID is the session identifier from the Entire-Session trailer
   550→	SessionID string
   551→
   552→	// MetadataDir is the metadata directory path from trailers
   553→	MetadataDir string
   554→
   555→	// IsTaskCheckpoint indicates if this is a task checkpoint
   556→	IsTaskCheckpoint bool
   557→
   558→	// ToolUseID is the tool use ID for task checkpoints
   559→	ToolUseID string
   560→
   561→	// Timestamp is when the checkpoint was created
   562→	Timestamp time.Time
