    34→// errStopIteration is used to stop commit iteration early in GetCheckpointAuthor.
    35→var errStopIteration = errors.New("stop iteration")
    36→
    37→// WriteCommitted writes a committed checkpoint to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    38→// Checkpoints are stored at sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    39→//
    40→// For task checkpoints (IsTask=true), additional files are written under tasks/<tool-use-id>/:
    41→//   - For incremental checkpoints: checkpoints/NNN-<tool-use-id>.json
    42→//   - For final checkpoints: checkpoint.json and agent-<agent-id>.jsonl
    43→func (s *GitStore) WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error {
    44→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
    45→
    46→	// Validate identifiers to prevent path traversal and malformed data
    47→	if opts.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
    48→		return errors.New("invalid checkpoint options: checkpoint ID is required")
    49→	}
    50→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(opts.SessionID); err != nil {
    51→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→	if err := validation.ValidateToolUseID(opts.ToolUseID); err != nil {
    54→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→	if err := validation.ValidateAgentID(opts.AgentID); err != nil {
    57→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
    61→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
    62→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
    66→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		return err
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	// Use sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    72→	basePath := opts.CheckpointID.Path() + "/"
    73→
    74→	// Track task metadata path for commit trailer
    75→	var taskMetadataPath string
    76→
    77→	// Handle task checkpoints
    78→	if opts.IsTask && opts.ToolUseID != "" {
    79→		taskMetadataPath, err = s.writeTaskCheckpointEntries(opts, basePath, entries)
    80→		if err != nil {
    81→			return err
    82→		}
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	// Write standard checkpoint entries (transcript, prompts, context, metadata)
    86→	if err := s.writeStandardCheckpointEntries(opts, basePath, entries); err != nil {
    87→		return err
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	// Build and commit
    91→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
    92→	if err != nil {
    93→		return err
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	commitMsg := s.buildCommitMessage(opts, taskMetadataPath)
    97→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, opts.AuthorName, opts.AuthorEmail)
    98→	if err != nil {
    99→		return err
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   103→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
   104→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   105→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	return nil
   109→}
   110→
   111→// getSessionsBranchEntries returns the sessions branch reference and flattened tree entries.
   112→func (s *GitStore) getSessionsBranchEntries() (*plumbing.Reference, map[string]object.TreeEntry, error) {
   113→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   114→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get sessions branch reference: %w", err)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	parentCommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   120→	if err != nil {
   121→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	baseTree, err := parentCommit.Tree()
   125→	if err != nil {
   126→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   130→	if err := FlattenTree(s.repo, baseTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   131→		return nil, nil, err
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	return ref, entries, nil
   135→}
   136→
   137→// writeTaskCheckpointEntries writes task-specific checkpoint entries and returns the task metadata path.
   138→func (s *GitStore) writeTaskCheckpointEntries(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   139→	taskPath := basePath + "tasks/" + opts.ToolUseID + "/"
   140→
   141→	if opts.IsIncremental {
   142→		return s.writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint(opts, taskPath, entries)
   143→	}
   144→	return s.writeFinalTaskCheckpoint(opts, taskPath, entries)
   145→}
   146→
   147→// writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint writes an incremental checkpoint file during task execution.
   148→func (s *GitStore) writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   149→	incData, err := redact.JSONLBytes(opts.IncrementalData)
   150→	if err != nil {
   151→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to redact incremental checkpoint: %w", err)
   152→	}
   153→	checkpoint := incrementalCheckpointData{
   154→		Type:      opts.IncrementalType,
   155→		ToolUseID: opts.ToolUseID,
   156→		Timestamp: time.Now().UTC(),
   157→		Data:      incData,
   158→	}
   159→	cpData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
   160→	if err != nil {
   161→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal incremental checkpoint: %w", err)
   162→	}
   163→	cpBlobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, cpData)
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create incremental checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	cpFilename := fmt.Sprintf("%03d-%s.json", opts.IncrementalSequence, opts.ToolUseID)
   169→	cpPath := taskPath + "checkpoints/" + cpFilename
   170→	entries[cpPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   171→		Name: cpPath,
   172→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   173→		Hash: cpBlobHash,
   174→	}
   175→	return cpPath, nil
   176→}
   177→
   178→// writeFinalTaskCheckpoint writes the final checkpoint.json and subagent transcript.
   179→func (s *GitStore) writeFinalTaskCheckpoint(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   180→	checkpoint := taskCheckpointData{
   181→		SessionID:      opts.SessionID,
   182→		ToolUseID:      opts.ToolUseID,
   183→		CheckpointUUID: opts.CheckpointUUID,
   184→		AgentID:        opts.AgentID,
   185→	}
   186→	checkpointData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
   187→	if err != nil {
   188→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal task checkpoint: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→	blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, checkpointData)
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create task checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	checkpointFile := taskPath + "checkpoint.json"
   196→	entries[checkpointFile] = object.TreeEntry{
   197→		Name: checkpointFile,
   198→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   199→		Hash: blobHash,
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	// Write subagent transcript if available
   203→	if opts.SubagentTranscriptPath != "" && opts.AgentID != "" {
   204→		agentContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.SubagentTranscriptPath)
   205→		if readErr == nil {
   206→			agentContent, readErr = redact.JSONLBytes(agentContent)
   207→		}
   208→		if readErr == nil {
   209→			agentBlobHash, agentBlobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, agentContent)
   210→			if agentBlobErr == nil {
   211→				agentPath := taskPath + "agent-" + opts.AgentID + ".jsonl"
   212→				entries[agentPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   213→					Name: agentPath,
   214→					Mode: filemode.Regular,
   215→					Hash: agentBlobHash,
   216→				}
   217→			}
   218→		}
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	// Return task path without trailing slash
   222→	return taskPath[:len(taskPath)-1], nil
   223→}
   224→
   225→// writeStandardCheckpointEntries writes session files to numbered subdirectories and
   226→// maintains a CheckpointSummary at the root level with aggregated statistics.
   227→//
   228→// Structure:
   229→//
   230→//	basePath/
   231→//	├── metadata.json         # CheckpointSummary (aggregated stats)
   232→//	├── 1/                    # First session
   233→//	│   ├── metadata.json     # CommittedMetadata (session-specific, includes initial_attribution)
   234→//	│   ├── full.jsonl
   235→//	│   ├── prompt.txt
   236→//	│   ├── context.md
   237→//	│   └── content_hash.txt
   238→//	├── 2/                    # Second session
   239→//	└── ...
   240→func (s *GitStore) writeStandardCheckpointEntries(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
   241→	// Read existing summary to get current session count
   242→	var existingSummary *CheckpointSummary
   243→	metadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
   244→	if entry, exists := entries[metadataPath]; exists {
   245→		existing, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   246→		if err == nil {
   247→			existingSummary = existing
   248→		}
   249→	}
   250→
   251→	// Determine session index: reuse existing slot if session ID matches, otherwise append
   252→	sessionIndex := s.findSessionIndex(basePath, existingSummary, entries, opts.SessionID)
   253→
   254→	// Write session files to numbered subdirectory
   255→	sessionPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/", basePath, sessionIndex)
   256→	sessionFilePaths, err := s.writeSessionToSubdirectory(opts, sessionPath, entries)
   257→	if err != nil {
   258→		return err
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	// Copy additional metadata files from directory if specified (to session subdirectory)
   262→	if opts.MetadataDir != "" {
   263→		if err := s.copyMetadataDir(opts.MetadataDir, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
   264→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy metadata directory: %w", err)
   265→		}
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Build the sessions array
   269→	var sessions []SessionFilePaths
   270→	if existingSummary != nil {
   271→		sessions = make([]SessionFilePaths, max(len(existingSummary.Sessions), sessionIndex+1))
   272→		copy(sessions, existingSummary.Sessions)
   273→	} else {
   274→		sessions = make([]SessionFilePaths, 1)
   275→	}
   276→	sessions[sessionIndex] = sessionFilePaths
   277→
   278→	// Update root metadata.json with CheckpointSummary
   279→	return s.writeCheckpointSummary(opts, basePath, entries, sessions)
   280→}
   281→
   282→// writeSessionToSubdirectory writes a single session's files to a numbered subdirectory.
   283→// Returns the absolute file paths from the git tree root for the sessions map.
   284→func (s *GitStore) writeSessionToSubdirectory(opts WriteCommittedOptions, sessionPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (SessionFilePaths, error) {
   285→	filePaths := SessionFilePaths{}
   286→
   287→	// Clear any existing entries at this path so stale files from a previous
   288→	// write (e.g. prompt.txt, context.md) don't persist on overwrite.
   289→	for key := range entries {
   290→		if strings.HasPrefix(key, sessionPath) {
   291→			delete(entries, key)
   292→		}
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	// Write transcript
   296→	if err := s.writeTranscript(opts, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
   297→		return filePaths, err
   298→	}
   299→	filePaths.Transcript = "/" + sessionPath + paths.TranscriptFileName
   300→	filePaths.ContentHash = "/" + sessionPath + paths.ContentHashFileName
   301→
   302→	// Write prompts
   303→	if len(opts.Prompts) > 0 {
   304→		promptContent := redact.String(strings.Join(opts.Prompts, "\n\n---\n\n"))
   305→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(promptContent))
   306→		if err != nil {
   307→			return filePaths, err
   308→		}
   309→		entries[sessionPath+paths.PromptFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   310→			Name: sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName,
   311→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   312→			Hash: blobHash,
   313→		}
   314→		filePaths.Prompt = "/" + sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	// Write context
   318→	if len(opts.Context) > 0 {
   319→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, redact.Bytes(opts.Context))
   320→		if err != nil {
   321→			return filePaths, err
   322→		}
   323→		entries[sessionPath+paths.ContextFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   324→			Name: sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName,
   325→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   326→			Hash: blobHash,
   327→		}
   328→		filePaths.Context = "/" + sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName
   329→	}
   330→
   331→	// Write session-level metadata.json (CommittedMetadata with all fields including initial_attribution)
   332→	sessionMetadata := CommittedMetadata{
   333→		CheckpointID:                opts.CheckpointID,
   334→		SessionID:                   opts.SessionID,
   335→		Strategy:                    opts.Strategy,
   336→		CreatedAt:                   time.Now().UTC(),
   337→		Branch:                      opts.Branch,
   338→		CheckpointsCount:            opts.CheckpointsCount,
   339→		FilesTouched:                opts.FilesTouched,
   340→		Agent:                       opts.Agent,
   341→		IsTask:                      opts.IsTask,
   342→		ToolUseID:                   opts.ToolUseID,
   343→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: opts.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   344→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   opts.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   345→		TranscriptLinesAtStart:      opts.CheckpointTranscriptStart, // Deprecated: kept for backward compat
   346→		TokenUsage:                  opts.TokenUsage,
   347→		InitialAttribution:          opts.InitialAttribution,
   348→		Summary:                     opts.Summary,
   349→	}
   350→
   351→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(sessionMetadata, "", "  ")
   352→	if err != nil {
   353→		return filePaths, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session metadata: %w", err)
   354→	}
   355→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   356→	if err != nil {
   357→		return filePaths, err
   358→	}
   359→	entries[sessionPath+paths.MetadataFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   360→		Name: sessionPath + paths.MetadataFileName,
   361→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   362→		Hash: metadataHash,
   363→	}
   364→	filePaths.Metadata = "/" + sessionPath + paths.MetadataFileName
   365→
   366→	return filePaths, nil
   367→}
   368→
   369→// writeCheckpointSummary writes the root-level CheckpointSummary with aggregated statistics.
   370→// sessions is the complete sessions array (already built by the caller).
   371→func (s *GitStore) writeCheckpointSummary(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry, sessions []SessionFilePaths) error {
   372→	checkpointsCount, filesTouched, tokenUsage, err :=
   373→		s.reaggregateFromEntries(basePath, len(sessions), entries)
   374→	if err != nil {
   375→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to aggregate session stats: %w", err)
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	summary := CheckpointSummary{
   379→		CheckpointID:     opts.CheckpointID,
   380→		Strategy:         opts.Strategy,
   381→		Branch:           opts.Branch,
   382→		CheckpointsCount: checkpointsCount,
   383→		FilesTouched:     filesTouched,
   384→		Sessions:         sessions,
   385→		TokenUsage:       tokenUsage,
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(summary, "", "  ")
   389→	if err != nil {
   390→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal checkpoint summary: %w", err)
   391→	}
   392→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   393→	if err != nil {
   394→		return err
   395→	}
   396→	entries[basePath+paths.MetadataFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   397→		Name: basePath + paths.MetadataFileName,
   398→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   399→		Hash: metadataHash,
   400→	}
   401→	return nil
   402→}
   403→
   404→// findSessionIndex returns the index of an existing session with the given ID,
   405→// or the next available index if not found. This prevents duplicate session entries.
   406→func (s *GitStore) findSessionIndex(basePath string, existingSummary *CheckpointSummary, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry, sessionID string) int {
   407→	if existingSummary == nil {
   408→		return 0
   409→	}
   410→	for i := range len(existingSummary.Sessions) {
   411→		path := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
   412→		if entry, exists := entries[path]; exists {
   413→			meta, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   414→			if err != nil {
   415→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read session metadata during dedup check",
   416→					slog.Int("session_index", i),
   417→					slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   418→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   419→				)
   420→				continue
   421→			}
   422→			if meta.SessionID == sessionID {
   423→				return i
   424→			}
   425→		}
   426→	}
   427→	return len(existingSummary.Sessions)
   428→}
   429→
   430→// reaggregateFromEntries reads all session metadata from the entries map and
   431→// reaggregates CheckpointsCount, FilesTouched, and TokenUsage.
   432→func (s *GitStore) reaggregateFromEntries(basePath string, sessionCount int, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (int, []string, *agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   433→	var totalCount int
   434→	var allFiles []string
   435→	var totalTokens *agent.TokenUsage
   436→
   437→	for i := range sessionCount {
   438→		path := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
   439→		entry, exists := entries[path]
   440→		if !exists {
   441→			return 0, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("session %d metadata not found at %s", i, path)
   442→		}
   443→		meta, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   444→		if err != nil {
   445→			return 0, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session %d metadata: %w", i, err)
   446→		}
   447→		totalCount += meta.CheckpointsCount
   448→		allFiles = mergeFilesTouched(allFiles, meta.FilesTouched)
   449→		totalTokens = aggregateTokenUsage(totalTokens, meta.TokenUsage)
   450→	}
   451→
   452→	return totalCount, allFiles, totalTokens, nil
   453→}
   454→
   455→// readJSONFromBlob reads JSON from a blob hash and decodes it to the given type.
   456→func readJSONFromBlob[T any](repo *git.Repository, hash plumbing.Hash) (*T, error) {
   457→	blob, err := repo.BlobObject(hash)
   458→	if err != nil {
   459→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get blob: %w", err)
   460→	}
   461→
   462→	reader, err := blob.Reader()
   463→	if err != nil {
   464→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get blob reader: %w", err)
   465→	}
   466→	defer reader.Close()
   467→
   468→	var result T
   469→	if err := json.NewDecoder(reader).Decode(&result); err != nil {
   470→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to decode: %w", err)
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	return &result, nil
   474→}
   475→
   476→// readSummaryFromBlob reads CheckpointSummary from a blob hash.
   477→func (s *GitStore) readSummaryFromBlob(hash plumbing.Hash) (*CheckpointSummary, error) {
   478→	return readJSONFromBlob[CheckpointSummary](s.repo, hash)
   479→}
   480→
   481→// aggregateTokenUsage sums two TokenUsage structs.
   482→// Returns nil if both inputs are nil.
   483→func aggregateTokenUsage(a, b *agent.TokenUsage) *agent.TokenUsage {
   484→	if a == nil && b == nil {
   485→		return nil
   486→	}
   487→	result := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   488→	if a != nil {
   489→		result.InputTokens = a.InputTokens
   490→		result.CacheCreationTokens = a.CacheCreationTokens
   491→		result.CacheReadTokens = a.CacheReadTokens
   492→		result.OutputTokens = a.OutputTokens
   493→		result.APICallCount = a.APICallCount
   494→	}
   495→	if b != nil {
   496→		result.InputTokens += b.InputTokens
   497→		result.CacheCreationTokens += b.CacheCreationTokens
   498→		result.CacheReadTokens += b.CacheReadTokens
   499→		result.OutputTokens += b.OutputTokens
   500→		result.APICallCount += b.APICallCount
   501→	}
   502→	return result
   503→}
   504→
   505→// writeTranscript writes the transcript file from in-memory content or file path.
   506→// If the transcript exceeds MaxChunkSize, it's split into multiple chunk files.
   507→func (s *GitStore) writeTranscript(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
   508→	transcript := opts.Transcript
   509→	if len(transcript) == 0 && opts.TranscriptPath != "" {
   510→		var readErr error
   511→		transcript, readErr = os.ReadFile(opts.TranscriptPath)
   512→		if readErr != nil {
   513→			// Non-fatal: transcript may not exist yet
   514→			transcript = nil
   515→		}
   516→	}
   517→	if len(transcript) == 0 {
   518→		return nil
   519→	}
   520→
   521→	// Redact secrets before chunking so content hash reflects redacted content
   522→	transcript, err := redact.JSONLBytes(transcript)
   523→	if err != nil {
   524→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to redact transcript secrets: %w", err)
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	// Chunk the transcript if it's too large
   528→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkTranscript(transcript, opts.Agent)
   529→	if err != nil {
   530→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk transcript: %w", err)
   531→	}
   532→
   533→	// Write chunk files
   534→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
   535→		chunkPath := basePath + agent.ChunkFileName(paths.TranscriptFileName, i)
   536→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, chunk)
   537→		if err != nil {
   538→			return err
   539→		}
   540→		entries[chunkPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   541→			Name: chunkPath,
   542→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   543→			Hash: blobHash,
   544→		}
   545→	}
   546→
   547→	// Content hash for deduplication (hash of full transcript)
   548→	contentHash := fmt.Sprintf("sha256:%x", sha256.Sum256(transcript))
   549→	hashBlob, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(contentHash))
   550→	if err != nil {
   551→		return err
   552→	}
   553→	entries[basePath+paths.ContentHashFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   554→		Name: basePath + paths.ContentHashFileName,
   555→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   556→		Hash: hashBlob,
   557→	}
   558→	return nil
   559→}
   560→
   561→// mergeFilesTouched combines two file lists, removing duplicates.
   562→func mergeFilesTouched(existing, additional []string) []string {
   563→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   564→	var result []string
   565→
   566→	for _, f := range existing {
   567→		if !seen[f] {
   568→			seen[f] = true
   569→			result = append(result, f)
   570→		}
   571→	}
   572→	for _, f := range additional {
   573→		if !seen[f] {
   574→			seen[f] = true
   575→			result = append(result, f)
   576→		}
   577→	}
   578→
   579→	sort.Strings(result)
   580→	return result
   581→}
   582→
   583→// readMetadataFromBlob reads CommittedMetadata from a blob hash.
   584→func (s *GitStore) readMetadataFromBlob(hash plumbing.Hash) (*CommittedMetadata, error) {
   585→	return readJSONFromBlob[CommittedMetadata](s.repo, hash)
   586→}
   587→
   588→// buildCommitMessage constructs the commit message with proper trailers.
   589→// The commit subject is always "Checkpoint: <id>" for consistency.
   590→// If CommitSubject is provided (e.g., for task checkpoints), it's included in the body.
   591→func (s *GitStore) buildCommitMessage(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskMetadataPath string) string {
   592→	var commitMsg strings.Builder
   593→
   594→	// Subject line is always the checkpoint ID for consistent formatting
   595→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Checkpoint: %s\n\n", opts.CheckpointID))
   596→
   597→	// Include custom description in body if provided (e.g., task checkpoint details)
   598→	if opts.CommitSubject != "" {
   599→		commitMsg.WriteString(opts.CommitSubject + "\n\n")
   600→	}
   601→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.SessionTrailerKey, opts.SessionID))
   602→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.StrategyTrailerKey, opts.Strategy))
   603→	if opts.Agent != "" {
   604→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.AgentTrailerKey, opts.Agent))
   605→	}
   606→	if opts.EphemeralBranch != "" {
   607→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.EphemeralBranchTrailerKey, opts.EphemeralBranch))
   608→	}
   609→	if taskMetadataPath != "" {
   610→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataPath))
   611→	}
   612→
   613→	return commitMsg.String()
   614→}
   615→
   616→// incrementalCheckpointData represents an incremental checkpoint during subagent execution.
   617→// This mirrors strategy.SubagentCheckpoint but avoids import cycles.
   618→type incrementalCheckpointData struct {
   619→	Type      string          `json:"type"`
   620→	ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   621→	Timestamp time.Time       `json:"timestamp"`
   622→	Data      json.RawMessage `json:"data"`
   623→}
   624→
   625→// taskCheckpointData represents a final task checkpoint.
   626→// This mirrors strategy.TaskCheckpoint but avoids import cycles.
   627→type taskCheckpointData struct {
   628→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   629→	ToolUseID      string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   630→	CheckpointUUID string `json:"checkpoint_uuid"`
   631→	AgentID        string `json:"agent_id,omitempty"`
   632→}
   633→
   634→// ReadCommitted reads a committed checkpoint's summary by ID from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   635→// Returns only the CheckpointSummary (paths + aggregated stats), not actual content.
   636→// Use ReadSessionContent to read actual transcript/prompts/context.
   637→// Returns nil, nil if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   638→//
   639→// The storage format uses numbered subdirectories for each session (0-based):
   640→//
   641→//	<checkpoint-id>/
   642→//	├── metadata.json      # CheckpointSummary with sessions map
   643→//	├── 0/                 # First session
   644→//	│   ├── metadata.json  # Session-specific metadata
   645→//	│   └── full.jsonl     # Transcript
   646→//	├── 1/                 # Second session
   647→//	└── ...
   648→func (s *GitStore) ReadCommitted(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*CheckpointSummary, error) {
   649→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   650→
   651→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   652→	if err != nil {
   653→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // No sessions branch means no checkpoint exists
   654→	}
   655→
   656→	checkpointPath := checkpointID.Path()
   657→	checkpointTree, err := tree.Tree(checkpointPath)
   658→	if err != nil {
   659→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // Checkpoint directory not found
   660→	}
   661→
   662→	// Read root metadata.json as CheckpointSummary
   663→	metadataFile, err := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName)
   664→	if err != nil {
   665→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // metadata.json not found
   666→	}
   667→
   668→	content, err := metadataFile.Contents()
   669→	if err != nil {
   670→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read metadata.json: %w", err)
   671→	}
   672→
   673→	var summary CheckpointSummary
   674→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err != nil {
   675→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse metadata.json: %w", err)
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	return &summary, nil
   679→}
   680→
   681→// ReadSessionContent reads the actual content for a specific session within a checkpoint.
   682→// sessionIndex is 0-based (0 for first session, 1 for second, etc.).
   683→// Returns the session's metadata, transcript, prompts, and context.
   684→// Returns an error if the checkpoint or session doesn't exist.
   685→func (s *GitStore) ReadSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionIndex int) (*SessionContent, error) {
   686→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   687→
   688→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   689→	if err != nil {
   690→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	checkpointPath := checkpointID.Path()
   694→	checkpointTree, err := tree.Tree(checkpointPath)
   695→	if err != nil {
   696→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   697→	}
   698→
   699→	// Get the session subdirectory
   700→	sessionDir := strconv.Itoa(sessionIndex)
   701→	sessionTree, err := checkpointTree.Tree(sessionDir)
   702→	if err != nil {
   703→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("session %d not found: %w", sessionIndex, err)
   704→	}
   705→
   706→	result := &SessionContent{}
   707→
   708→	// Read session-specific metadata
   709→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   710→	if metadataFile, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   711→		if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   712→			if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &result.Metadata); jsonErr == nil {
   713→				agentType = result.Metadata.Agent
   714→			}
   715→		}
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	// Read transcript
   719→	if transcript, transcriptErr := readTranscriptFromTree(sessionTree, agentType); transcriptErr == nil && transcript != nil {
   720→		result.Transcript = transcript
   721→	}
   722→
   723→	// Read prompts
   724→	if file, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.PromptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   725→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   726→			result.Prompts = content
   727→		}
   728→	}
   729→
   730→	// Read context
   731→	if file, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.ContextFileName); fileErr == nil {
   732→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   733→			result.Context = content
   734→		}
   735→	}
   736→
   737→	return result, nil
   738→}
   739→
   740→// ReadLatestSessionContent is a convenience method that reads the latest session's content.
   741→// This is equivalent to ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, len(summary.Sessions)-1).
   742→func (s *GitStore) ReadLatestSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*SessionContent, error) {
   743→	summary, err := s.ReadCommitted(ctx, checkpointID)
   744→	if err != nil {
   745→		return nil, err
   746→	}
   747→	if summary == nil {
   748→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   749→	}
   750→	if len(summary.Sessions) == 0 {
   751→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint has no sessions: %s", checkpointID)
   752→	}
   753→
   754→	latestIndex := len(summary.Sessions) - 1
   755→	return s.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, latestIndex)
   756→}
   757→
   758→// ReadSessionContentByID reads a session's content by its session ID.
   759→// This is useful when you have the session ID but don't know its index within the checkpoint.
   760→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   761→// Returns an error if no session with the given ID exists in the checkpoint.
   762→func (s *GitStore) ReadSessionContentByID(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string) (*SessionContent, error) {
   763→	summary, err := s.ReadCommitted(ctx, checkpointID)
   764→	if err != nil {
   765→		return nil, err
   766→	}
   767→	if summary == nil {
   768→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   769→	}
   770→
   771→	// Iterate through sessions to find the one with matching session ID
   772→	for i := range len(summary.Sessions) {
   773→		content, readErr := s.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)
   774→		if readErr != nil {
   775→			continue
   776→		}
   777→		if content != nil && content.Metadata.SessionID == sessionID {
   778→			return content, nil
   779→		}
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("session %q not found in checkpoint %s", sessionID, checkpointID)
   783→}
   784→
   785→// ListCommitted lists all committed checkpoints from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   786→// Scans sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/ directories containing metadata.json.
   787→//
   788→
   789→func (s *GitStore) ListCommitted(ctx context.Context) ([]CommittedInfo, error) {
   790→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   791→
   792→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   793→	if err != nil {
   794→		return []CommittedInfo{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // No sessions branch means empty list
   795→	}
   796→
   797→	var checkpoints []CommittedInfo
   798→
   799→	// Scan sharded structure: <2-char-prefix>/<remaining-id>/metadata.json
   800→	for _, bucketEntry := range tree.Entries {
   801→		if bucketEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   802→			continue
   803→		}
   804→		// Bucket should be 2 hex chars
   805→		if len(bucketEntry.Name) != 2 {
   806→			continue
   807→		}
   808→
   809→		bucketTree, treeErr := s.repo.TreeObject(bucketEntry.Hash)
   810→		if treeErr != nil {
   811→			continue
   812→		}
   813→
   814→		// Each entry in the bucket is the remaining part of the checkpoint ID
   815→		for _, checkpointEntry := range bucketTree.Entries {
   816→			if checkpointEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   817→				continue
   818→			}
   819→
   820→			checkpointTree, cpTreeErr := s.repo.TreeObject(checkpointEntry.Hash)
   821→			if cpTreeErr != nil {
   822→				continue
   823→			}
   824→
   825→			// Reconstruct checkpoint ID: <bucket><remaining>
   826→			checkpointIDStr := bucketEntry.Name + checkpointEntry.Name
   827→			checkpointID, cpIDErr := id.NewCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   828→			if cpIDErr != nil {
   829→				// Skip invalid checkpoint IDs (shouldn't happen with our own data)
   830→				continue
   831→			}
   832→
   833→			info := CommittedInfo{
   834→				CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   835→			}
   836→
   837→			// Get details from root metadata file (CheckpointSummary format)
   838→			if metadataFile, fileErr := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   839→				if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   840→					var summary CheckpointSummary
   841→					if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err == nil {
   842→						info.CheckpointsCount = summary.CheckpointsCount
   843→						info.FilesTouched = summary.FilesTouched
   844→						info.SessionCount = len(summary.Sessions)
   845→
   846→						// Read session metadata from latest session to get Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt
   847→						if len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   848→							latestIndex := len(summary.Sessions) - 1
   849→							latestDir := strconv.Itoa(latestIndex)
   850→							if sessionTree, treeErr := checkpointTree.Tree(latestDir); treeErr == nil {
   851→								if sessionMetadataFile, smErr := sessionTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); smErr == nil {
   852→									if sessionContent, scErr := sessionMetadataFile.Contents(); scErr == nil {
   853→										var sessionMetadata CommittedMetadata
   854→										if json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &sessionMetadata) == nil {
   855→											info.Agent = sessionMetadata.Agent
   856→											info.SessionID = sessionMetadata.SessionID
   857→											info.CreatedAt = sessionMetadata.CreatedAt
   858→										}
   859→									}
   860→								}
   861→							}
   862→						}
   863→					}
   864→				}
   865→			}
   866→
   867→			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, info)
   868→		}
   869→	}
   870→
   871→	// Sort by time (most recent first)
   872→	sort.Slice(checkpoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   873→		return checkpoints[i].CreatedAt.After(checkpoints[j].CreatedAt)
   874→	})
   875→
   876→	return checkpoints, nil
   877→}
   878→
   879→// GetTranscript retrieves the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
   880→// Returns the latest session's transcript.
   881→func (s *GitStore) GetTranscript(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
   882→	content, err := s.ReadLatestSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID)
   883→	if err != nil {
   884→		return nil, err
   885→	}
   886→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   887→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   888→	}
   889→	return content.Transcript, nil
   890→}
   891→
   892→// GetSessionLog retrieves the session transcript and session ID for a checkpoint.
   893→// This is the primary method for looking up session logs by checkpoint ID.
   894→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   895→// Returns ErrNoTranscript if the checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
   896→func (s *GitStore) GetSessionLog(cpID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, string, error) {
   897→	content, err := s.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID)
   898→	if err != nil {
   899→		if errors.Is(err, ErrCheckpointNotFound) {
   900→			return nil, "", ErrCheckpointNotFound
   901→		}
   902→		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   903→	}
   904→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   905→		return nil, "", ErrNoTranscript
   906→	}
   907→	return content.Transcript, content.Metadata.SessionID, nil
   908→}
   909→
   910→// LookupSessionLog is a convenience function that opens the repository and retrieves
   911→// a session log by checkpoint ID. This is the primary entry point for callers that
   912→// don't already have a GitStore instance.
   913→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   914→// Returns ErrNoTranscript if the checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
   915→func LookupSessionLog(cpID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, string, error) {
   916→	repo, err := git.PlainOpenWithOptions(".", &git.PlainOpenOptions{DetectDotGit: true})
   917→	if err != nil {
   918→		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   919→	}
   920→	store := NewGitStore(repo)
   921→	return store.GetSessionLog(cpID)
   922→}
   923→
   924→// UpdateSummary updates the summary field in the latest session's metadata.
   925→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   926→func (s *GitStore) UpdateSummary(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *Summary) error {
   927→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   928→
   929→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
   930→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
   931→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
   932→	}
   933→
   934→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
   935→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
   936→	if err != nil {
   937→		return err
   938→	}
   939→
   940→	// Read root CheckpointSummary to find the latest session
   941→	basePath := checkpointID.Path() + "/"
   942→	rootMetadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
   943→	entry, exists := entries[rootMetadataPath]
   944→	if !exists {
   945→		return ErrCheckpointNotFound
   946→	}
   947→
   948→	checkpointSummary, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   949→	if err != nil {
   950→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint summary: %w", err)
   951→	}
   952→
   953→	// Find the latest session's metadata path (0-based indexing)
   954→	latestIndex := len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) - 1
   955→	sessionMetadataPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, latestIndex, paths.MetadataFileName)
   956→	sessionEntry, exists := entries[sessionMetadataPath]
   957→	if !exists {
   958→		return fmt.Errorf("session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
   959→	}
   960→
   961→	// Read and update session metadata
   962→	existingMetadata, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(sessionEntry.Hash)
   963→	if err != nil {
   964→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read session metadata: %w", err)
   965→	}
   966→
   967→	// Update the summary
   968→	existingMetadata.Summary = summary
   969→
   970→	// Write updated session metadata
   971→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(existingMetadata, "", "  ")
   972→	if err != nil {
   973→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal metadata: %w", err)
   974→	}
   975→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   976→	if err != nil {
   977→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create metadata blob: %w", err)
   978→	}
   979→	entries[sessionMetadataPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   980→		Name: sessionMetadataPath,
   981→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   982→		Hash: metadataHash,
   983→	}
   984→
   985→	// Build and commit
   986→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
   987→	if err != nil {
   988→		return err
   989→	}
   990→
   991→	authorName, authorEmail := getGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
   992→	commitMsg := fmt.Sprintf("Update summary for checkpoint %s (session: %s)", checkpointID, existingMetadata.SessionID)
   993→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)
   994→	if err != nil {
   995→		return err
   996→	}
   997→
   998→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   999→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
  1000→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1001→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1002→	}
  1003→
  1004→	return nil
  1005→}
  1006→
  1007→// ensureSessionsBranch ensures the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists.
  1008→func (s *GitStore) ensureSessionsBranch() error {
  1009→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1010→	_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1011→	if err == nil {
  1012→		return nil // Branch exists
  1013→	}
  1014→
  1015→	// Create orphan branch with empty tree
  1016→	emptyTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, make(map[string]object.TreeEntry))
  1017→	if err != nil {
  1018→		return err
  1019→	}
  1020→
  1021→	authorName, authorEmail := getGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
  1022→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, "Initialize sessions branch", authorName, authorEmail)
  1023→	if err != nil {
  1024→		return err
  1025→	}
  1026→
  1027→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
  1028→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1029→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1030→	}
  1031→	return nil
  1032→}
  1033→
  1034→// getSessionsBranchTree returns the tree object for the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
  1035→// Falls back to origin/entire/checkpoints/v1 if the local branch doesn't exist.
  1036→func (s *GitStore) getSessionsBranchTree() (*object.Tree, error) {
  1037→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1038→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1039→	if err != nil {
  1040→		// Local branch doesn't exist, try remote-tracking branch
  1041→		remoteRefName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1042→		ref, err = s.repo.Reference(remoteRefName, true)
  1043→		if err != nil {
  1044→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("sessions branch not found: %w", err)
  1045→		}
  1046→	}
  1047→
  1048→	commit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1049→	if err != nil {
  1050→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
  1051→	}
  1052→
  1053→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1054→	if err != nil {
  1055→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
  1056→	}
  1057→
  1058→	return tree, nil
  1059→}
  1060→
  1061→// CreateBlobFromContent creates a blob object from in-memory content.
  1062→// Exported for use by strategy package (session_test.go)
  1063→func CreateBlobFromContent(repo *git.Repository, content []byte) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
  1064→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
  1065→	obj.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
  1066→	obj.SetSize(int64(len(content)))
  1067→
  1068→	writer, err := obj.Writer()
  1069→	if err != nil {
  1070→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get object writer: %w", err)
  1071→	}
  1072→
  1073→	_, err = writer.Write(content)
  1074→	if err != nil {
  1075→		_ = writer.Close()
  1076→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write blob content: %w", err)
  1077→	}
  1078→	if err := writer.Close(); err != nil {
  1079→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to close blob writer: %w", err)
  1080→	}
  1081→
  1082→	hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
  1083→	if err != nil {
  1084→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store blob object: %w", err)
  1085→	}
  1086→	return hash, nil
  1087→}
  1088→
  1089→// copyMetadataDir copies all files from a directory to the checkpoint path.
  1090→// Used to include additional metadata files like task checkpoints, subagent transcripts, etc.
  1091→func (s *GitStore) copyMetadataDir(metadataDir, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
  1092→	err := filepath.Walk(metadataDir, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
  1093→		if err != nil {
  1094→			return err
  1095→		}
  1096→		if info.IsDir() {
  1097→			return nil
  1098→		}
  1099→
  1100→		// Skip symlinks to prevent reading files outside the metadata directory.
  1101→		// A symlink could point to sensitive files (e.g., /etc/passwd) which would
  1102→		// then be captured in the checkpoint and stored in git history.
  1103→		if info.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 {
  1104→			return nil
  1105→		}
  1106→
  1107→		// Get relative path within metadata dir
  1108→		relPath, err := filepath.Rel(metadataDir, path)
  1109→		if err != nil {
  1110→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to get relative path for %s: %w", path, err)
  1111→		}
  1112→
  1113→		// Prevent path traversal via symlinks pointing outside the metadata dir
  1114→		if strings.HasPrefix(relPath, "..") {
  1115→			return fmt.Errorf("path traversal detected: %s", relPath)
  1116→		}
  1117→
  1118→		// Create blob from file with secrets redaction
  1119→		blobHash, mode, err := createRedactedBlobFromFile(s.repo, path, relPath)
  1120→		if err != nil {
  1121→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob for %s: %w", path, err)
  1122→		}
  1123→
  1124→		// Store at checkpoint path
  1125→		fullPath := basePath + relPath
  1126→		entries[fullPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1127→			Name: fullPath,
  1128→			Mode: mode,
  1129→			Hash: blobHash,
  1130→		}
  1131→
  1132→		return nil
  1133→	})
  1134→	if err != nil {
  1135→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to walk metadata directory: %w", err)
  1136→	}
  1137→	return nil
  1138→}
  1139→
  1140→// createRedactedBlobFromFile reads a file, applies secrets redaction, and creates a git blob.
  1141→// JSONL files get JSONL-aware redaction; all other files get plain string redaction.
  1142→func createRedactedBlobFromFile(repo *git.Repository, filePath, treePath string) (plumbing.Hash, filemode.FileMode, error) {
  1143→	info, err := os.Stat(filePath)
  1144→	if err != nil {
  1145→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to stat file: %w", err)
  1146→	}
  1147→
  1148→	mode := filemode.Regular
  1149→	if info.Mode()&0o111 != 0 {
  1150→		mode = filemode.Executable
  1151→	}
  1152→
  1153→	content, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // filePath comes from walking the metadata directory
  1154→	if err != nil {
  1155→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read file: %w", err)
  1156→	}
  1157→
  1158→	// Skip redaction for binary files — they can't contain text secrets and
  1159→	// running string replacement on them would corrupt the data.
  1160→	isBin, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(content))
  1161→	if binErr != nil || isBin {
  1162→		hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1163→		if err != nil {
  1164→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1165→		}
  1166→		return hash, mode, nil
  1167→	}
  1168→
  1169→	if strings.HasSuffix(treePath, ".jsonl") {
  1170→		content, err = redact.JSONLBytes(content)
  1171→		if err != nil {
  1172→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to redact secrets: %w", err)
  1173→		}
  1174→	} else {
  1175→		content = redact.Bytes(content)
  1176→	}
  1177→
  1178→	hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1179→	if err != nil {
  1180→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1181→	}
  1182→	return hash, mode, nil
  1183→}
  1184→
  1185→// getGitAuthorFromRepo retrieves the git user.name and user.email from the repository config.
  1186→func getGitAuthorFromRepo(repo *git.Repository) (name, email string) {
  1187→	// Get repository config (includes local settings)
  1188→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
  1189→	if err == nil {
  1190→		name = cfg.User.Name
  1191→		email = cfg.User.Email
  1192→	}
  1193→
  1194→	// Provide sensible defaults if git user is not configured
  1195→	if name == "" {
  1196→		name = "Unknown"
  1197→	}
  1198→	if email == "" {
  1199→		email = "unknown@local"
  1200→	}
  1201→
  1202→	return name, email
  1203→}
  1204→
  1205→// readTranscriptFromTree reads a transcript from a git tree, handling both chunked and non-chunked formats.
  1206→// It checks for chunk files first (.001, .002, etc.), then falls back to the base file.
  1207→// The agentType is used for reassembling chunks in the correct format.
  1208→func readTranscriptFromTree(tree *object.Tree, agentType agent.AgentType) ([]byte, error) {
  1209→	// Collect all transcript-related files
  1210→	var chunkFiles []string
  1211→	var hasBaseFile bool
  1212→
  1213→	for _, entry := range tree.Entries {
  1214→		if entry.Name == paths.TranscriptFileName || entry.Name == paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy {
  1215→			hasBaseFile = true
  1216→		}
  1217→		// Check for chunk files (full.jsonl.001, full.jsonl.002, etc.)
  1218→		if strings.HasPrefix(entry.Name, paths.TranscriptFileName+".") {
  1219→			idx := agent.ParseChunkIndex(entry.Name, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1220→			if idx > 0 {
  1221→				chunkFiles = append(chunkFiles, entry.Name)
  1222→			}
  1223→		}
  1224→	}
  1225→
  1226→	// If we have chunk files, read and reassemble them
  1227→	if len(chunkFiles) > 0 {
  1228→		// Sort chunk files by index
  1229→		chunkFiles = agent.SortChunkFiles(chunkFiles, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1230→
  1231→		// Check if base file should be included as chunk 0.
  1232→		// NOTE: This assumes the chunking convention where the unsuffixed file
  1233→		// (full.jsonl) is chunk 0, and numbered files (.001, .002) are chunks 1+.
  1234→		if hasBaseFile {
  1235→			chunkFiles = append([]string{paths.TranscriptFileName}, chunkFiles...)
  1236→		}
  1237→
  1238→		var chunks [][]byte
  1239→		for _, chunkFile := range chunkFiles {
  1240→			file, err := tree.File(chunkFile)
  1241→			if err != nil {
  1242→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read transcript chunk file from tree",
  1243→					slog.String("chunk_file", chunkFile),
  1244→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1245→				)
  1246→				continue
  1247→			}
  1248→			content, err := file.Contents()
  1249→			if err != nil {
  1250→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read transcript chunk contents",
  1251→					slog.String("chunk_file", chunkFile),
  1252→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1253→				)
  1254→				continue
  1255→			}
  1256→			chunks = append(chunks, []byte(content))
  1257→		}
  1258→
  1259→		if len(chunks) > 0 {
  1260→			result, err := agent.ReassembleTranscript(chunks, agentType)
  1261→			if err != nil {
  1262→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to reassemble transcript: %w", err)
  1263→			}
  1264→			return result, nil
  1265→		}
  1266→	}
  1267→
  1268→	// Fall back to reading base file (non-chunked or backwards compatibility)
  1269→	if file, err := tree.File(paths.TranscriptFileName); err == nil {
  1270→		if content, err := file.Contents(); err == nil {
  1271→			return []byte(content), nil
  1272→		}
  1273→	}
  1274→
  1275→	// Try legacy filename
  1276→	if file, err := tree.File(paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); err == nil {
  1277→		if content, err := file.Contents(); err == nil {
  1278→			return []byte(content), nil
  1279→		}
  1280→	}
  1281→
  1282→	return nil, nil
  1283→}
  1284→
  1285→// Author contains author information for a checkpoint.
  1286→type Author struct {
  1287→	Name  string
  1288→	Email string
  1289→}
  1290→
  1291→// GetCheckpointAuthor retrieves the author of a checkpoint from the entire/checkpoints/v1 commit history.
  1292→// Returns the author of the commit that introduced this checkpoint's metadata.json file.
  1293→// Returns empty Author if the checkpoint is not found or the sessions branch doesn't exist.
  1294→func (s *GitStore) GetCheckpointAuthor(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (Author, error) {
  1295→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
  1296→
  1297→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1298→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1299→	if err != nil {
  1300→		return Author{}, nil
  1301→	}
  1302→
  1303→	// Path to the checkpoint's metadata file
  1304→	metadataPath := checkpointID.Path() + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName
  1305→
  1306→	// Walk commit history looking for the commit that introduced this file
  1307→	iter, err := s.repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
  1308→		From:  ref.Hash(),
  1309→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
  1310→	})
  1311→	if err != nil {
  1312→		return Author{}, nil
  1313→	}
  1314→	defer iter.Close()
  1315→
  1316→	var author Author
  1317→	var foundCommit *object.Commit
  1318→
  1319→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
  1320→		tree, treeErr := c.Tree()
  1321→		if treeErr != nil {
  1322→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip commits we can't read, continue searching
  1323→		}
  1324→
  1325→		_, fileErr := tree.File(metadataPath)
  1326→		if fileErr != nil {
  1327→			// File doesn't exist in this commit - we've gone past the creation point
  1328→			if foundCommit != nil {
  1329→				return errStopIteration
  1330→			}
  1331→			return nil
  1332→		}
  1333→
  1334→		// File exists - track it (oldest one with file is the creator)
  1335→		foundCommit = c
  1336→		author = Author{
  1337→			Name:  c.Author.Name,
  1338→			Email: c.Author.Email,
  1339→		}
  1340→		return nil
  1341→	})
  1342→
  1343→	// Ignore errStopIteration - it's just for early exit
  1344→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStopIteration) {
  1345→		return Author{}, nil
  1346→	}
  1347→
  1348→	return author, nil
  1349→}

    19→const (
    20→	// SessionStateDirName is the directory name for session state files within git common dir.
    21→	SessionStateDirName = "entire-sessions"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// State represents the state of an active session.
    25→// This is stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
    26→type State struct {
    27→	// SessionID is the unique session identifier
    28→	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
    29→
    30→	// BaseCommit tracks the current shadow branch base. Initially set to HEAD when the
    31→	// session starts, but updated on migration (pull/rebase) and after condensation.
    32→	// Used for shadow branch naming and checkpoint storage — NOT for attribution.
    33→	BaseCommit string `json:"base_commit"`
    34→
    35→	// AttributionBaseCommit is the commit used as the reference point for attribution calculations.
    36→	// Unlike BaseCommit (which tracks the shadow branch and moves with migration), this field
    37→	// preserves the original base commit so deferred condensation can correctly calculate
    38→	// agent vs human line attribution. Updated only after successful condensation.
    39→	AttributionBaseCommit string `json:"attribution_base_commit,omitempty"`
    40→
    41→	// WorktreePath is the absolute path to the worktree root
    42→	WorktreePath string `json:"worktree_path,omitempty"`
    43→
    44→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
    45→	// Derived from .git/worktrees/<name>/, stable across git worktree move
    46→	WorktreeID string `json:"worktree_id,omitempty"`
    47→
    48→	// StartedAt is when the session was started
    49→	StartedAt time.Time `json:"started_at"`
    50→
    51→	// EndedAt is when the session was explicitly closed by the user.
    52→	// nil means the session is still active or was not cleanly closed.
    53→	EndedAt *time.Time `json:"ended_at,omitempty"`
    54→
    55→	// Phase is the lifecycle stage of this session (see phase.go).
    56→	// Empty means idle (backward compat with pre-state-machine files).
    57→	Phase Phase `json:"phase,omitempty"`
    58→
    59→	// PendingCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID for the current commit cycle.
    60→	// Generated once when first needed, reused across all commits in the session.
    61→	PendingCheckpointID string `json:"pending_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
    62→
    63→	// LastInteractionTime is updated on every hook invocation.
    64→	// Used for stale session detection in "entire sessions fix".
    65→	LastInteractionTime *time.Time `json:"last_interaction_time,omitempty"`
    66→
    67→	// StepCount is the number of checkpoints/steps created in this session.
    68→	// JSON tag kept as "checkpoint_count" for backward compatibility with existing state files.
    69→	StepCount int `json:"checkpoint_count"`
    70→
    71→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart is the transcript line offset where the current
    72→	// checkpoint cycle began. Set to 0 at session start, updated to current
    73→	// transcript length after each condensation. Used to scope the transcript
    74→	// for checkpoint condensation: "everything since last checkpoint".
    75→	CheckpointTranscriptStart int `json:"checkpoint_transcript_start,omitempty"`
    76→
    77→	// Deprecated: CondensedTranscriptLines is replaced by CheckpointTranscriptStart.
    78→	// Kept for backward compatibility with existing state files.
    79→	// Use NormalizeAfterLoad() to migrate.
    80→	CondensedTranscriptLines int `json:"condensed_transcript_lines,omitempty"`
    81→
    82→	// UntrackedFilesAtStart tracks files that existed at session start (to preserve during rewind)
    83→	UntrackedFilesAtStart []string `json:"untracked_files_at_start,omitempty"`
    84→
    85→	// FilesTouched tracks files modified/created/deleted during this session
    86→	FilesTouched []string `json:"files_touched,omitempty"`
    87→
    88→	// LastCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID from last condensation, reused for subsequent commits without new content
    89→	LastCheckpointID id.CheckpointID `json:"last_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
    90→
    91→	// AgentType identifies the agent that created this session (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI", "Cursor")
    92→	AgentType agent.AgentType `json:"agent_type,omitempty"`
    93→
    94→	// Token usage tracking (accumulated across all checkpoints in this session)
    95→	TokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage `json:"token_usage,omitempty"`
    96→
    97→	// Deprecated: TranscriptLinesAtStart is replaced by CheckpointTranscriptStart.
    98→	// Kept for backward compatibility with existing state files.
    99→	TranscriptLinesAtStart int `json:"transcript_lines_at_start,omitempty"`
   100→
   101→	// TranscriptIdentifierAtStart is the last transcript identifier when the session started.
   102→	// Used for identifier-based transcript scoping (UUID for Claude, message ID for Gemini).
   103→	TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string `json:"transcript_identifier_at_start,omitempty"`
   104→
   105→	// TranscriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection)
   106→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path,omitempty"`
   107→
   108→	// FirstPrompt is the first user prompt that started this session (truncated for display)
   109→	FirstPrompt string `json:"first_prompt,omitempty"`
   110→
   111→	// PromptAttributions tracks user and agent line changes at each prompt start.
   112→	// This enables accurate attribution by capturing user edits between checkpoints.
   113→	PromptAttributions []PromptAttribution `json:"prompt_attributions,omitempty"`
   114→
   115→	// PendingPromptAttribution holds attribution calculated at prompt start (before agent runs).
   116→	// This is moved to PromptAttributions when SaveChanges is called.
   117→	PendingPromptAttribution *PromptAttribution `json:"pending_prompt_attribution,omitempty"`
   118→}
   119→
   120→// PromptAttribution captures line-level attribution data at the start of each prompt.
   121→// By recording what changed since the last checkpoint BEFORE the agent works,
   122→// we can accurately separate user edits from agent contributions.
   123→type PromptAttribution struct {
   124→	// CheckpointNumber is which checkpoint this was recorded before (1-indexed)
   125→	CheckpointNumber int `json:"checkpoint_number"`
   126→
   127→	// UserLinesAdded is lines added by user since the last checkpoint
   128→	UserLinesAdded int `json:"user_lines_added"`
   129→
   130→	// UserLinesRemoved is lines removed by user since the last checkpoint
   131→	UserLinesRemoved int `json:"user_lines_removed"`
   132→
   133→	// AgentLinesAdded is total agent lines added so far (base → last checkpoint).
   134→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   135→	AgentLinesAdded int `json:"agent_lines_added"`
   136→
   137→	// AgentLinesRemoved is total agent lines removed so far (base → last checkpoint).
   138→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   139→	AgentLinesRemoved int `json:"agent_lines_removed"`
   140→
   141→	// UserAddedPerFile tracks per-file user additions for accurate modification tracking.
   142→	// This enables distinguishing user self-modifications from agent modifications.
   143→	// See docs/architecture/attribution.md for details.
   144→	UserAddedPerFile map[string]int `json:"user_added_per_file,omitempty"`
   145→}
   146→
   147→// NormalizeAfterLoad applies backward-compatible migrations to state loaded from disk.
   148→// Call this after deserializing a State from JSON.
   149→func (s *State) NormalizeAfterLoad() {
   150→	// Migrate transcript fields: CheckpointTranscriptStart replaces both
   151→	// CondensedTranscriptLines and TranscriptLinesAtStart from older state files.
   152→	if s.CheckpointTranscriptStart == 0 {
   153→		if s.CondensedTranscriptLines > 0 {
   154→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.CondensedTranscriptLines
   155→		} else if s.TranscriptLinesAtStart > 0 {
   156→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.TranscriptLinesAtStart
   157→		}
   158→	}
   159→	// Clear deprecated fields so they aren't re-persisted.
   160→	// Note: this is a one-way migration. If the state is re-saved, older CLI versions
   161→	// will see 0 for these fields and fall back to scoping from the transcript start.
   162→	// This is acceptable since CLI upgrades are monotonic and the worst case is
   163→	// redundant transcript content in a condensation, not data loss.
   164→	s.CondensedTranscriptLines = 0
   165→	s.TranscriptLinesAtStart = 0
   166→
   167→	// Backfill AttributionBaseCommit for sessions created before this field existed.
   168→	// Without this, a mid-turn commit would migrate BaseCommit and the fallback in
   169→	// calculateSessionAttributions would use the migrated value, producing zero attribution.
   170→	if s.AttributionBaseCommit == "" && s.BaseCommit != "" {
   171→		s.AttributionBaseCommit = s.BaseCommit
   172→	}
   173→}
   174→
   175→// StateStore provides low-level operations for managing session state files.
   176→//
   177→// StateStore is a primitive for session state persistence. It is NOT the same as
   178→// the Sessions interface - it only handles state files in .git/entire-sessions/,
   179→// not the full session data which includes checkpoint content.
   180→//
   181→// Use StateStore directly in strategies for performance-critical state operations.
   182→// Use the Sessions interface (when implemented) for high-level session management.
   183→type StateStore struct {
   184→	// stateDir is the directory where session state files are stored
   185→	stateDir string
   186→}
   187→
   188→// NewStateStore creates a new state store.
   189→// Uses the git common dir to store session state (shared across worktrees).
   190→func NewStateStore() (*StateStore, error) {
   191→	commonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()
   192→	if err != nil {
   193→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   194→	}
   195→	return &StateStore{
   196→		stateDir: filepath.Join(commonDir, SessionStateDirName),
   197→	}, nil
   198→}
   199→
   200→// NewStateStoreWithDir creates a new state store with a custom directory.
   201→// This is useful for testing.
   202→func NewStateStoreWithDir(stateDir string) *StateStore {
   203→	return &StateStore{stateDir: stateDir}
   204→}
   205→
   206→// Load loads the session state for the given session ID.
   207→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
   208→func (s *StateStore) Load(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) (*State, error) {
   209→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   210→
   211→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   212→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   213→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   217→
   218→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID
   219→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   220→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
   221→	}
   222→	if err != nil {
   223→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	var state State
   227→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   228→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
   229→	}
   230→	state.NormalizeAfterLoad()
   231→	return &state, nil
   232→}
   233→
   234→// Save saves the session state atomically.
   235→func (s *StateStore) Save(ctx context.Context, state *State) error {
   236→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   237→
   238→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   239→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
   240→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	if err := os.MkdirAll(s.stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   244→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   248→	if err != nil {
   249→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
   250→	}
   251→
   252→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(state.SessionID)
   253→
   254→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
   255→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
   256→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   257→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
   258→	}
   259→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
   260→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
   261→	}
   262→	return nil
   263→}
   264→
   265→// Clear removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   266→func (s *StateStore) Clear(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) error {
   267→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   268→
   269→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   270→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   271→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   275→
   276→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   277→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   278→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   279→		}
   280→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   281→	}
   282→	return nil
   283→}
   284→
   285→// RemoveAll removes the entire session state directory.
   286→// This is used during uninstall to completely remove all session state.
   287→func (s *StateStore) RemoveAll() error {
   288→	if err := os.RemoveAll(s.stateDir); err != nil {
   289→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state directory: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→	return nil
   292→}
   293→
   294→// List returns all session states.
   295→func (s *StateStore) List(ctx context.Context) ([]*State, error) {
   296→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   297→
   298→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(s.stateDir)
   299→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   300→		return nil, nil
   301→	}
   302→	if err != nil {
   303→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	var states []*State
   307→	for _, entry := range entries {
   308→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
   309→			continue
   310→		}
   311→		if strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".tmp") {
   312→			continue // Skip temp files
   313→		}
   314→
   315→		sessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
   316→		state, err := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   317→		if err != nil {
   318→			continue // Skip corrupted state files
   319→		}
   320→		if state == nil {
   321→			continue
   322→		}
   323→
   324→		states = append(states, state)
   325→	}
   326→	return states, nil
   327→}
   328→
   329→// stateFilePath returns the path to a session state file.
   330→func (s *StateStore) stateFilePath(sessionID string) string {
   331→	return filepath.Join(s.stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   332→}
   333→
   334→// getGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   335→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   336→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   337→func getGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   338→	ctx := context.Background()
   339→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   340→	cmd.Dir = "."
   341→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   342→	if err != nil {
   343→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   347→
   348→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   349→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   350→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   351→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   352→	}
   353→
   354→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   355→}
   356→

    15→
    16→// Shadow strategy session state methods.
    17→// Uses session.StateStore for persistence.
    18→
    19→// loadSessionState loads session state using the StateStore.
    20→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) loadSessionState(sessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
    21→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		return nil, err
    24→	}
    25→	state, err := store.Load(context.Background(), sessionID)
    26→	if err != nil {
    27→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→	return state, nil
    30→}
    31→
    32→// saveSessionState saves session state using the StateStore.
    33→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) saveSessionState(state *SessionState) error {
    34→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return err
    37→	}
    38→	if err := store.Save(context.Background(), state); err != nil {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}
    43→
    44→// clearSessionState clears session state using the StateStore.
    45→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) clearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
    46→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		return err
    49→	}
    50→	if err := store.Clear(context.Background(), sessionID); err != nil {
    51→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→	return nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→// listAllSessionStates returns all active session states.
    57→// It filters out orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists.
    58→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listAllSessionStates() ([]*SessionState, error) {
    59→	store, err := s.getStateStore()
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get state store: %w", err)
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	sessionStates, err := store.List(context.Background())
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	if len(sessionStates) == 0 {
    70→		return nil, nil
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	var states []*SessionState
    79→	for _, sessionState := range sessionStates {
    80→		state := sessionState
    81→
    82→		// Skip and cleanup orphaned sessions whose shadow branch no longer exists.
    83→		// Keep active sessions (shadow branch may not be created yet) and sessions
    84→		// with LastCheckpointID (needed for checkpoint ID reuse on subsequent commits).
    85→		// Clean up everything else: stale pre-state-machine sessions (empty phase),
    86→		// IDLE/ENDED sessions that were never condensed, etc.
    87→		shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    88→		refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    89→		if _, err := repo.Reference(refName, true); err != nil {
    90→			if !state.Phase.IsActive() && state.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
    91→				//nolint:errcheck,gosec // G104: Cleanup is best-effort, shouldn't fail the list operation
    92→				store.Clear(context.Background(), state.SessionID)
    93→				continue
    94→			}
    95→		}
    96→
    97→		states = append(states, state)
    98→	}
    99→	return states, nil
   100→}
   101→
   102→// findSessionsForWorktree finds all sessions for the given worktree path.
   103→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   104→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   105→	if err != nil {
   106→		return nil, err
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	var matching []*SessionState
   110→	for _, state := range allStates {
   111→		if state.WorktreePath == worktreePath {
   112→			matching = append(matching, state)
   113→		}
   114→	}
   115→	return matching, nil
   116→}
   117→
   118→// findSessionsForCommit finds all sessions where base_commit matches the given SHA.
   119→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   120→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   121→	if err != nil {
   122→		return nil, err
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	var matching []*SessionState
   126→	for _, state := range allStates {
   127→		if state.BaseCommit == baseCommitSHA {
   128→			matching = append(matching, state)
   129→		}
   130→	}
   131→	return matching, nil
   132→}
   133→
   134→// FindSessionsForCommit is the exported version of findSessionsForCommit.
   135→// Used by the rewind reset command to find sessions to clean up.
   136→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) FindSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA string) ([]*SessionState, error) {
   137→	return s.findSessionsForCommit(baseCommitSHA)
   138→}
   139→
   140→// ClearSessionState is the exported version of clearSessionState.
   141→// Used by the rewind reset command to clean up session state files.
   142→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) ClearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
   143→	return s.clearSessionState(sessionID)
   144→}
   145→
   146→// CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints counts how many other active sessions
   147→// from the SAME worktree (different from currentSessionID) have created checkpoints
   148→// on the SAME base commit (current HEAD). This is used to show an informational message
   149→// about concurrent sessions that will be included in the next commit.
   150→// Returns 0, nil if no such sessions exist.
   151→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (int, error) {
   152→	currentWorktree, err := GetWorktreePath()
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		return 0, err
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	// Get current HEAD to compare with session base commits
   158→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return 0, err
   161→	}
   162→	head, err := repo.Head()
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   165→	}
   166→	currentHead := head.Hash().String()
   167→
   168→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return 0, err
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	count := 0
   174→	for _, state := range allStates {
   175→		// Only consider sessions from the same worktree with checkpoints
   176→		// AND based on the same commit (current HEAD)
   177→		// Sessions from different base commits are independent and shouldn't be counted
   178→		if state.SessionID != currentSessionID &&
   179→			state.WorktreePath == currentWorktree &&
   180→			state.StepCount > 0 &&
   181→			state.BaseCommit == currentHead {
   182→			count++
   183→		}
   184→	}
   185→	return count, nil
   186→}
   187→
   188→// initializeSession creates a new session state or updates a partial one.
   189→// A partial state may exist if the concurrent session warning was shown.
   190→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   191→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   192→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   193→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) initializeSession(repo *git.Repository, sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) (*SessionState, error) {
   194→	head, err := repo.Head()
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree path: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Get worktree ID for shadow branch naming
   205→	worktreeID, err := paths.GetWorktreeID(worktreePath)
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree ID: %w", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Capture untracked files at session start to preserve them during rewind
   211→	untrackedFiles, err := collectUntrackedFiles()
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		// Non-fatal: continue even if we can't collect untracked files
   214→		untrackedFiles = nil
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	now := time.Now()
   218→	headHash := head.Hash().String()
   219→	state := &SessionState{
   220→		SessionID:             sessionID,
   221→		BaseCommit:            headHash,
   222→		AttributionBaseCommit: headHash,
   223→		WorktreePath:          worktreePath,
   224→		WorktreeID:            worktreeID,
   225→		StartedAt:             now,
   226→		LastInteractionTime:   &now,
   227→		StepCount:             0,
   228→		UntrackedFilesAtStart: untrackedFiles,
   229→		AgentType:             agentType,
   230→		TranscriptPath:        transcriptPath,
   231→		FirstPrompt:           truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt),
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   235→		return nil, err
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	return state, nil
   239→}
   240→
   241→// getShadowBranchNameForCommit returns the shadow branch name for the given base commit and worktree ID.
   242→// worktreeID should be empty for the main worktree or the internal git worktree name for linked worktrees.
   243→func getShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID string) string {
   244→	return checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   245→}

     1→package strategy
